  > 731 | ### **Think-Tank Migration Plan**  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 732 | 
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 733 | #### **Overview**  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 734 | We are migrating the **To-Do module** to **Think-Tank**, with the following changes:  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 735 | - **All data will be moved to MongoDB.**  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 736 | - **The module will be renamed to "Think-Tank".**  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 737 | - **New fields and features will be introduced to improve organization and tracking.**  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 738 | 
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 739 | ---
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 740 | 
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 741 | ### **Key Changes**  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 742 | 
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 743 | #### **1. Data Structure Updates**  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 744 | Each Think-Tank item will have the following fields:  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 745 | - **\`description\`** – Contains a **smartContent** description.  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 746 | - **\`flags\`** – Labels like **Urgent, Important, Groomed**.  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 747 | - **\`type\`** – Defines the category of an item. Possible values:  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 748 |   - \`To-do\`  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 749 |   - \`Raw-question\`  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 750 |   - \`Yet-to-be-decided\` (default, can be changed anytime)  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 751 | - **\`inspiredFrom\`** – References other Think-Tank items.  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 752 |   - If Item C is inspired by Item A & B, then:  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 753 |     - **Item C** will list A & B in \`inspiredFrom\`.  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 754 |     - **Item A & B** will list C under \`inspirationCause\`.  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 755 |   - This logic will be refined as we implement it.  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 756 | 
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 757 | ---
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 758 | 
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 759 | #### **2. Access & Visibility**  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 760 | - **Think-Tank items are private by default.**  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 761 | - Only the **creator** can view them unless shared explicitly.  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 762 | - A **\`createdBy\`** field will track the owner.  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 763 |   - This will be included in API headers or another suitable mechanism during implementation.  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 764 | 
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 765 | ---
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 766 | 
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 767 | #### **3. "Why" Section for Auditability**  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 768 | - Every item should include a **"why" section** in its description.  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 769 | - **No separate field** is introduced to keep Think-Tank flexible and open-ended.  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 770 | - Items **may or may not** be completed before being marked **Closed**.  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 771 | 
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 772 | ---
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 773 | 
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 774 | ### **User Interface (UI) Enhancements**  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 775 | 
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 776 | #### **1. Pagination**  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 777 | - Default **page size: 1**.  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 778 | - Total item count will be displayed based on user-selected controls.  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 779 | 
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 780 | #### **2. Filters**  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 781 | Users can filter items by:  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 782 | - **Status**: Open / Closed / Both  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 783 | - **Type**: To-do, Raw-question, Yet-to-be-decided  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 784 |   - **At least one type must always be selected.**  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 785 |   - Users must select a different type before unchecking the last remaining selection.  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 786 | 
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 787 | #### **3. Page Size Customization**  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 788 | - Users can **set page size between 1-10**.  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 789 | - If an **invalid page size** is provided (not an integer or outside 1-10):  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 790 |   - A **warning panel** will be triggered (to be removed after the first release).  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 791 |   - Warnings will be tracked using a \`messages\` array with:  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 792 |     - **\`messageType\`**: Info | Warning | Error (default: Error)  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 793 |     - **\`message\`**: The message text.  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 794 |   - The system will default to **page size = 1** in such cases.  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 795 | 
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 796 | #### **4. Sorting**  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 797 | - Users can apply **zero or more sorting options**.  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 798 | - Default sorting: **Newest first (reverse chronological order of \`createdDate\`).**  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 799 | - Sorting rules will be stored in an array of objects:  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 800 |   \`\`\`ts
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 801 |   { sortBy: string; direction: "ASC" | "DESC" }
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 802 |   \`\`\`  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 803 | - Sorting parameters will be reflected in the **URL route or location state** (to be decided during implementation).  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 804 | 
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 805 | ---

        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 887 | 
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 888 | #### **1. What is the difference between a \`to-do\`, \`task\`, \`goal\`, and \`long-term goal\`?**  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 889 | 
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 890 | - **To-Do**:  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 891 |   - A raw idea or task that may or may not be executed later.  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 892 |   - Private to the user unless shared.  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 893 |   - Cannot be linked to other modules.  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 894 |   - Should include a **"why" section** before closing to clarify its purpose.  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 895 |   - Users must assign a reviewer for accountability.  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 896 | 
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 897 | - **Task**:  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 898 |   - A well-defined piece of work that can be completed in hours or days.  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 899 |   - Visible in other TweetApp modules.  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 900 |   - Can be linked to Topics, Tweets, Questions, Tags, or future modules.  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 901 | 
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 902 | - **Goal**:  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 903 |   - A larger work unit that takes **weeks** to complete.  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 904 |   - Private to the user unless shared.  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 905 |   - Contains multiple **Tasks**.  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 906 | 
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 907 | - **Long-Term Goal**:  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 908 |   - A significant objective that also takes **weeks** to accomplish.  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 909 |   - Private to the user unless shared.  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 910 |   - Must include a **plan (memory maps)** for execution.  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 911 |   - Contains multiple **Goals**.  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 912 | 
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 913 | ### **Hierarchy Summary**  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 914 | 🔹 **Long-Term Goal** → Contains multiple **Goals**  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 915 | 🔹 **Goal** → Contains multiple **Tasks**  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 916 | 🔹 **Task** → Standalone unit of work (linked to other modules)  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 917 | 🔹 **To-Do** → Private note, cannot be linked to other modules  
        | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

  > 1246 | - Allocate time based on importance—focus more on areas that need improvement.  
         | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

         | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 1248 | 
         | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

         | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 1250 | - Instead of long study sessions, revise in **short bursts** (30-45 minutes).  
         | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 1251 | - Use the **Pomodoro Technique** (25 mins study + 5 mins break) to stay focused.  
         | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 1252 | - Summarize key learnings daily in a **personal knowledge base** (Notion, OneNote, or Obsidian).  
         | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 1253 | 
         | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

  > 1256 | - Maintain a **"Lessons Learned" journal** to note challenges and solutions.

         | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 1258 |

  > 1261 | - Watch short tutorial videos during lunch breaks.  
         | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 1262 | - Use flashcards (Anki, Quizlet) for quick topic recall.  
         | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 1263 | 
         | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

         | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

         | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 1266 | - Use a **habit tracker** to monitor progress and stay accountable.  
         | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 1267 | - Every weekend, **reflect** on what worked and adjust the schedule if needed.  
         | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  > 1268 | 
         | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
